Mathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.HomEquiv

ComplexShape.Embedding.HasLiftdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.HomEquiv, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $x_{4}$ be a homomorphism of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $L$. Then $\operatorname{HasLift}$ holds for $e$ and $x_{4}$, if for all elements $j$ of $x$, if $\operatorname{BoundaryGE}$ holds for $e$ and $j$, then for all elements $\mathrm{i'}$ of $x_{1}$, if $\operatorname{Rel}$ holds for $\mathrm{c'}$, $\mathrm{i'}$ and $\operatorname{f}$ applied to $e$ and $j$, then the composition of $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $K$, $\mathrm{i'}$ and $\operatorname{f}$ applied to $e$ and $j$ and $\operatorname{f}$ applied to $x_{4}$ and $j$ is equal to $0$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), x_{4} \in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), L)\vdash \operatorname{HasLift} (e , x_{4}):\Leftrightarrow \forall j \in x . \operatorname{BoundaryGE} (e , j)\rightarrow (\forall \mathrm{i'} \in x_{1} . \operatorname{Rel} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{i'} , \operatorname{f} (e , j))\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{d} (K , \mathrm{i'} , \operatorname{f} (e , j)), \operatorname{f} (x_{4} , j))}{0})$
French
Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $x_{4}$ un homomorphisme de la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et $L$. Alors $\operatorname{HasLift}$ tient pour $e$ et $x_{4}$ si pour tous les éléments $j$ de $x$, si $\operatorname{BoundaryGE}$ tient pour $e$ et $j$, alors pour tous les éléments $\mathrm{i'}$ de $x_{1}$, si $\operatorname{Rel}$ tient pour $\mathrm{c'}$, $\mathrm{i'}$ et $\operatorname{f}$ appliqué à $e$ et $j$, alors la composition de $\operatorname{d}$ appliqué à $K$, $\mathrm{i'}$ et $\operatorname{f}$ appliqué à $e$ et $j$ et $\operatorname{f}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$ et $j$ est égale à $0$.
ComplexShape.Embedding.homEquivdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.HomEquiv, function or constant
Wording
the homomorphism equivalence of K and L (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $L$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Then the homomorphism equivalence of $K$ and $L$ is an equivalence from the set of homomorphisms of $K$ and the extension of $L$ and $e$ to $\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{\operatorname{HasLift} (e , x_{4})}$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), L \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c)\vdash \operatorname{homEquiv} (e , K , L)\in \EquivMACRoAL {\operatorname{Hom} (K , \operatorname{extend} (L , e))}{(\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{\operatorname{HasLift} (e , x_{4})})}$
French
Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $L$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $c$. Alors l'équivalence d'homomorphismes de $K$ et $L$ est une équivalence de l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de $K$ et l'extension de $L$ et $e$ à $\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{\operatorname{HasLift} (e , x_{4})}$.
ComplexShape.Embedding.homRestrictdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.HomEquiv, function or constant
Wording
the homomorphism restriction of e and x4 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $x_{4}$ be a homomorphism of $K$ and the extension of $L$ and $e$. Then the homomorphism restriction of $e$ and $x_{4}$ is a homomorphism of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $L$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), x_{4} \in \operatorname{Hom} (K , \operatorname{extend} (L , e))\vdash \operatorname{homRestrict} (e , x_{4})\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), L)$
French
Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $x_{4}$ un homomorphisme de $K$ et l'extension de $L$ et $e$. Alors la restriction d'homomorphismes de $e$ et $x_{4}$ est un homomorphisme de la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et $L$.
ComplexShape.Embedding.liftExtenddefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.HomEquiv, function or constant
Wording
the lift extension of e and x4 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $x_{4}$ be a homomorphism of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $L$ such that $\operatorname{HasLift}$ holds for $e$ and $x_{4}$. Then the lift extension of $e$ and $x_{4}$ is a homomorphism of $K$ and the extension of $L$ and $e$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the function that maps $\mathrm{i'}$ to $\operatorname{f}$ applied to $x_{4}$ and $\mathrm{i'}$ and the function that maps $x_{5}$, $x_{6}$ and $h_{3}$ to $\operatorname{comm}$ applied to $x_{4}$, $h_{2}$, $x_{5}$ and $x_{6}$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), x_{4} \in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), L), \operatorname{HasLift} (e , x_{4})\vdash \operatorname{liftExtend} (e , x_{4} , h_{2}):= \operatorname{mk} (\lambda \mathrm{i'} . \operatorname{f} (x_{4} , \mathrm{i'}), \lambda x_{5} , x_{6} , h_{3} . \operatorname{comm} (x_{4} , h_{2} , x_{5} , x_{6}))\in \operatorname{Hom} (K , \operatorname{extend} (L , e))$
French
Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $x_{4}$ un homomorphisme de la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et $L$ tel que $\operatorname{HasLift}$ tienne pour $e$ et $x_{4}$. Alors l'extension de relèvements de $e$ et $x_{4}$ est un homomorphisme de $K$ et l'extension de $L$ et $e$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $\mathrm{i'}$ à $\operatorname{f}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$ et $\mathrm{i'}$ et la fonction qui envoie $x_{5}$, $x_{6}$ et $h_{3}$ à $\operatorname{comm}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$, $h_{2}$, $x_{5}$ et $x_{6}$.
ComplexShape.Embedding.liftExtendfArrowIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.HomEquiv, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $x_{4}$ be a homomorphism of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $L$ such that $\operatorname{HasLift}$ holds for $e$ and $x_{4}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}$. Then $\operatorname{liftExtendfArrowIso}$ applied to $e$, $x_{4}$, $h_{2}$ and $\mathrm{hi}$ is an isomorphism of $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{f}$ applied to the lift extension of $e$ and $x_{4}$ and $\mathrm{i'}$ and $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{f}$ applied to $x_{4}$ and $i$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), x_{4} \in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), L), \operatorname{HasLift} (e , x_{4}), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}\vdash \operatorname{liftExtendfArrowIso} (e , x_{4} , h_{2} , \mathrm{hi})\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{f} (\operatorname{liftExtend} (e , x_{4} , h_{2}), \mathrm{i'})), \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{f} (x_{4} , i)))$
French
Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $x_{4}$ un homomorphisme de la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et $L$ tel que $\operatorname{HasLift}$ tienne pour $e$ et $x_{4}$. Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}$. Alors $\operatorname{liftExtendfArrowIso}$ appliqué à $e$, $x_{4}$, $h_{2}$ et $\mathrm{hi}$ est un isomorphisme de $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{f}$ appliqué à l'extension de relèvements de $e$ et $x_{4}$ et $\mathrm{i'}$ et $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{f}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$ et $i$.

HomologicalComplex.IsStrictlySupportedclassMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.IsSupported, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Then we can say that $\operatorname{IsStrictlySupported}$ holds for $K$ and $e$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'})\vdash \operatorname{IsStrictlySupported} (K , e)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\operatorname{IsStrictlySupported}$ tient pour $K$ et $e$.

Mathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.Restriction

ComplexShape.Embedding.restrictionFunctordefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.Restriction, function or constant
Wording
the restriction functor of e and C (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $C$ be a set. Then the restriction functor of $e$ and $C$ is a functor of the set of homological complexes of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$ and the set of homological complexes of $C$ and $c$.
Formula
$e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), C \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{restrictionFunctor} (e , C)\in \operatorname{Functor} (\mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c))$
French
Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $C$ un ensemble. Alors le foncteur de restrictions de $e$ et $C$ est un foncteur de l'ensemble de nombres complexes homologiques de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$ et l'ensemble de nombres complexes homologiques de $C$ et $c$.
HomologicalComplex.restrictiondefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.Restriction, function or constant
Wording
the restriction of K and e (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Then the restriction of $K$ and $e$ is a homological complex of $C$ and $c$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'})\vdash \operatorname{restriction} (K , e)\in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c)$
French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Alors la restriction de $K$ et $e$ est un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $c$.
HomologicalComplex.restrictionMapdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.Restriction, function or constant
Wording
the restriction map of x4 and e (from the name)
English
Let $x_{4}$ be a homomorphism of $K$ and $L$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Then the restriction map of $x_{4}$ and $e$ is a homomorphism of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and the restriction of $L$ and $e$.
Formula
$x_{4} \in \operatorname{Hom} (K , L), e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'})\vdash \operatorname{restrictionMap} (x_{4} , e)\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), \operatorname{restriction} (L , e))$
French
Soit $x_{4}$ un homomorphisme de $K$ et $L$. Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Alors l'application de restrictions de $x_{4}$ et $e$ est un homomorphisme de la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et la restriction de $L$ et $e$.
HomologicalComplex.restrictionXIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.Restriction, function or constant
Wording
the restriction isomorphism of K and e (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}$. Then the restriction isomorphism of $K$ and $e$ is an isomorphism of $\operatorname{X}$ applied to the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $i$ and $\operatorname{X}$ applied to $K$ and $\mathrm{i'}$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}\vdash \operatorname{restrictionXIso} (K , e , h_{1})\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{X} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), i), \operatorname{X} (K , \mathrm{i'}))$
French

Alors l'isomorphisme de restrictions de $K$ et $e$ est un isomorphisme de $\operatorname{X}$ appliqué à la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et $i$ et $\operatorname{X}$ appliqué à $K$ et $\mathrm{i'}$.

Mathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.RestrictionHomology

HomologicalComplex.restriction.sc'IsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.RestrictionHomology, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $i$, $j$ and $k$ be elements of $x$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}$ and $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{prev} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{i'}}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Then $\operatorname{sc'Iso}$ applied to $K$, $e$, $i$, $j$, $k$, $\mathrm{hi'}$, $\mathrm{hj'}$, $\mathrm{hk'}$, $\mathrm{hi''}$ and $\mathrm{hk''}$ is an isomorphism of $\operatorname{sc'}$ applied to the restriction of $K$ and $e$, $i$, $j$ and $k$ and $\operatorname{sc'}$ applied to $K$, $\mathrm{i'}$, $\mathrm{j'}$ and $\mathrm{k'}$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), i , j , k \in x , \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{prev} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{i'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}\vdash \operatorname{sc'Iso} (K , e , i , j , k , \mathrm{hi'} , \mathrm{hj'} , \mathrm{hk'} , \mathrm{hi''} , \mathrm{hk''})\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{sc'} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), i , j , k), \operatorname{sc'} (K , \mathrm{i'} , \mathrm{j'} , \mathrm{k'}))$
French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soient $i$, $j$ et $k$ des éléments de $x$ tels que $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}$ et $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{prev} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{i'}}$. Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Alors $\operatorname{sc'Iso}$ appliqué à $K$, $e$, $i$, $j$, $k$, $\mathrm{hi'}$, $\mathrm{hj'}$, $\mathrm{hk'}$, $\mathrm{hi''}$ et $\mathrm{hk''}$ est un isomorphisme de $\operatorname{sc'}$ appliqué à la restriction de $K$ et $e$, $i$, $j$ et $k$ et $\operatorname{sc'}$ appliqué à $K$, $\mathrm{i'}$, $\mathrm{j'}$ et $\mathrm{k'}$.
HomologicalComplex.restrictionCyclesIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.RestrictionHomology, function or constant
Wording
the restriction cycle isomorphism of j and k (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $j$ and $k$ be elements of $x$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (c , j)}{k}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}$ and $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Then the restriction cycle isomorphism of $j$ and $k$ is an isomorphism of the cycle of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $j$ and the cycle of $K$ and $\mathrm{j'}$.
Formula

French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soient $j$ et $k$ des éléments de $x$ tels que $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (c , j)}{k}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}$ et $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Alors l'isomorphisme de cycles de restrictions de $j$ et $k$ est un isomorphisme du cycle de la restriction de $K$ et $e$ et $j$ et le cycle de $K$ et $\mathrm{j'}$.
HomologicalComplex.restrictionHomologyIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.Embedding.RestrictionHomology, function or constant
Wording
the restriction homology isomorphism of j and k (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $e$ be an embedding of $c$ and $\mathrm{c'}$. Let $i$, $j$ and $k$ be elements of $x$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{prev} (c , j)}{i}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (c , j)}{k}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}$ and $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{prev} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{i'}}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}$. Then the restriction homology isomorphism of $j$ and $k$ is an isomorphism of the homology of the restriction of $K$ and $e$ and $j$ and the homology of $K$ and $\mathrm{j'}$ defined as the composition of $\operatorname{homologyIsoSc'}$ applied to the restriction of $K$ and $e$, $i$, $j$, $k$, $\mathrm{hi}$ and $\mathrm{hk}$ and the composition of the homology isomorphism of $\operatorname{sc'Iso}$ applied to $K$, $e$, $i$, $j$, $k$, $\mathrm{hi'}$, $\mathrm{hj'}$, $\mathrm{hk'}$, $\mathrm{hi''}$ and $\mathrm{hk''}$ and the inverse of $\operatorname{homologyIsoSc'}$ applied to $K$, $\mathrm{i'}$, $\mathrm{j'}$, $\mathrm{k'}$, $\mathrm{hi''}$ and $\mathrm{hk''}$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , \mathrm{c'}), e \in \operatorname{Embedding} (c , \mathrm{c'}), i , j , k \in x , \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{prev} (c , j)}{i}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{next} (c , j)}{k}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , i)}{\mathrm{i'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , j)}{\mathrm{j'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{f} (e , k)}{\mathrm{k'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{prev} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{i'}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{next} (\mathrm{c'} , \mathrm{j'})}{\mathrm{k'}}\vdash \operatorname{restrictionHomologyIso} (K , e , i , j , k , \mathrm{hi} , \mathrm{hk} , \mathrm{hi'} , \mathrm{hj'} , \mathrm{hk'} , \mathrm{hi''} , \mathrm{hk''}):= \operatorname{trans} (\operatorname{homologyIsoSc'} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), i , j , k , \mathrm{hi} , \mathrm{hk}), \operatorname{trans} (\operatorname{homologyMapIso} (\operatorname{sc'Iso} (K , e , i , j , k , \mathrm{hi'} , \mathrm{hj'} , \mathrm{hk'} , \mathrm{hi''} , \mathrm{hk''})), \operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{homologyIsoSc'} (K , \mathrm{i'} , \mathrm{j'} , \mathrm{k'} , \mathrm{hi''} , \mathrm{hk''}))))\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{homology} (\operatorname{restriction} (K , e), j), \operatorname{homology} (K , \mathrm{j'}))$
French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. Soit $e$ un plongement de $c$ et $\mathrm{c'}$. English
Let $P$ and $Q$ be chain complexes of $V$ and $\Natural$. Let $\mathrm{zero}$ be a homomorphism of $\operatorname{X}$ applied to $P$ and $0$ and $\operatorname{X}$ applied to $Q$ and $0$. Let $\mathrm{one}$ be a homomorphism of $\operatorname{X}$ applied to $P$ and $1$ and $\operatorname{X}$ applied to $Q$ and $1$ such that the composition of $\mathrm{one}$ and $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $Q$, $1$ and $0$ is equal to the composition of $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $P$, $1$ and $0$ and $\mathrm{zero}$. Let $\mathrm{succ}$ be a function from elements $n$ of $\Natural$ and elements $p$ of $\mathrm{PSigma}$ applied to the function that maps $f$ to $\mathrm{PSigma}$ applied to the function that maps $\mathrm{f'}$ to the proposition that the composition of $\mathrm{f'}$ and $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $Q$, $n + 1$ and $n$ is equal to the composition of $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $P$, $n + 1$ and $n$ and $f$ to elements of $\mathrm{PSigma}$ applied to the function that maps $\mathrm{f''}$ to the proposition that the composition of $\mathrm{f''}$ and $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $Q$, $n + 2$ and $n + 1$ is equal to the composition of $\operatorname{d}$ applied to $P$, $n + 2$ and $n + 1$ and $\operatorname{fst}$ applied to $\operatorname{snd}$ applied to $p$. Then the homomorphism of $\mathrm{one}$ and $n$ is a homomorphism of $P$ and $Q$.
